# REPORT: EMPLOYEES WITH "PEMBA" IN THEIR DEPARTMENT

**Tume ya Utumishi Serikalini — Civil Service Management System (CSMS)**

**Report Date:** June 2026

---
## 1. Executive Summary

This report identifies all employees whose **Department** field contains the word **"Pemba"** across all 17 institutions in the CSMS database. These employees work in various Pemba-based departments and offices (e.g. *Ofisi Kuu Pemba*, *OFISI YA URATIBU PEMBA*, *AFISI KUU PEMBA*, etc.) and are distributed across 34 institutions.

| Employees with "Pemba" in department | **3425** |
| Male | 1855 |
| Female | 1570 |
| Distinct department names containing "Pemba" | 17 |
| Institutions affected | 34 |

## 6. Key Observations

1. **Wizara ya Elimu na Mafunzo ya Amali** overwhelmingly dominates with **2,214 employees (64.6%)** — the vast majority of Pemba-based education personnel.
2. **Wizara ya Kilimo, Umwagiliaji, Maliasili na Mifugo** follows with **378 employees (11.0%)**, reflecting agricultural departments in Pemba.
3. **Wakala wa Barabara** has **168 employees** under Pemba coordination offices, mostly male (154 of 168), suggesting road construction/maintenance roles.
4. **Ofisi ya Mhasibu Mkuu wa Serikali** has 108 employees working from or coordinating Pemba financial operations.
5. Almost all employees (3,421 out of 3,425 = **99.9%**) have a **Confirmed** status.
6. The most common department designation is **"Ofisi Kuu Pemba"** (2,881 employees) — a generic label suggesting the Pemba main office for various institutions.
